# Umgesetzte Korrekturen aus dem Prüfbericht

> Grundlage: [`PRUEFBERICHT_INHALT_UEBERSETZUNG.md`](./PRUEFBERICHT_INHALT_UEBERSETZUNG.md). Gegenüberstellung alt → neu für DE und EN, je Datei aufklappbar.  
> ~~durchgestrichen~~ = vorher · **fett** = jetzt · ✅ = richtige Option · ⚠️ = Lösung geändert · 💬 = neue Erklärung

## Überblick

| | Anzahl |
| :-- | --: |
| Geänderte Fragen (DE und/oder EN) | **1341** |
| Geänderte Fragetexte | 530 |
| Geänderte Optionen | 4011 |
| Korrigierte Lösungen | 8 |
| Korrigierte Bibelstellen | 82 |
| Erklärungen neu/geändert/entfernt | 21 |

**Wichtigste Punkte**

- `en/44_apostelgeschichte.json` wurde vollständig neu aus der DE-Fassung übersetzt (alle 145 Fragen).
- Falsch markierte Lösungen korrigiert: `ps_061`, `ps_063`, `ps_074`, `apg_136` (DE und EN).
- Distraktoren, die ebenfalls richtig waren, durch eindeutig falsche ersetzt (u. a. 2. Mose, `1sam_011`, `1mose_019`).
- EN-Distraktoren durchgängig als Übersetzung der DE-Optionen mit gleichem Index angeglichen; EN-Namen in KJV-Schreibweise (Beth-el, Beer-sheba, Eben-ezer, Zacchæus …).
- DE-Zitate an den hinterlegten Grundtext (eigene Revision der Schlachter 1951 bzw. Luther 1912) angeglichen.
- Erklärungen nur dort neu angelegt, wo sie für das Verständnis der Lösung nötig sind.

#### `1mose_163`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Wie reagierte Joseph emotional, als er seinen leiblichen Bruder Benjamin erblickte?~~  
  → **Was sagte Joseph, als er Benjamin, den Sohn seiner Mutter, erblickte, und was tat er unmittelbar danach?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Er umarmte ihn sofort stürmisch vor allen Ägyptern und rief seinen Namen~~  
  → **„Willkommen in Ägypten!“; er ließ ihm mit seinem Wagen die königlichen Paläste zeigen**
- **Option 1:** ~~Er blieb völlig kalt und distanziert, um keinen Verdacht zu erregen~~  
  → **„Siehe, mein geliebter Bruder!“; er umarmte ihn unter Tränen vor allen Dienern**
- **Option 2:** ~~Er fragte ihn streng aus, ob der Vater noch am Leben sei~~  
  → **„Du sollst mein Mundschenk sein!“; er ließ ihm ein goldenes Gewand bringen**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~Sein Herz wühlte sich auf gegen seinen Bruder~~  
  → **„Gott sei dir gnädig, mein Sohn!“; er suchte einen Ort zum Weinen und ging in sein Gemach**

**EN**

- **Option 2:** ~~"Art thou indeed the youngest?"; he commanded a golden garment for him~~  
  → **"Thou shalt be my cupbearer"; he commanded a golden garment for him**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~"God be gracious unto thee, my son"; he sought where to weep, into his chamber~~  
  → **"God be gracious unto thee, my son"; he sought where to weep and entered into his chamber**

---

#### `1mose_165`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Why were Joseph's brethren astonished when they sat down to dine before him?~~  
  → **In what astonishing order were the brethren seated before Joseph?**
- **Option 0:** ~~They were served with gold vessels stolen from temple of Baal in Canaan~~  
  → **In a random order which the steward determined by lot**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~They sat the firstborn according to birthright, youngest according to youth~~  
  → **The firstborn according to his birthright, and the youngest according to his youth**
- **Option 2:** ~~They heard the servants sing ancient Hebrew songs taught in Beersheba~~  
  → **According to the value of the presents they had brought out of Canaan**
- **Option 3:** ~~They were offered sacrificial meat consecrated to Egyptian holy gods~~  
  → **The sons of Leah on the right, the sons of the handmaids on the left**

#### `1mose_225`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Warum bat Joseph das Haus des Pharao und nicht den Pharao selbst um Erlaubnis zur Reise nach Kanaan?~~  
  → **Über wen ließ Joseph den Pharao um Erlaubnis bitten, seinen Vater in Kanaan zu begraben?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Weil der Pharao schwer krank war und niemanden empfing~~  
  → **Über seine Brüder, die vor den Pharao traten**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Weil er sich in Trauerkleidung befand und sich das Haar nicht schor~~  
  → **Über das Haus des Pharao (seinen Hof)**
- **Option 2:** ~~Weil Joseph die ägyptische Sprache verlernt hatte~~  
  → **Über Potiphar, den Obersten der Leibwache**
- **Option 3:** ~~Weil er Angst hatte, der Pharao würde ihn wegen Hochverrats verhaften lassen~~  
  → **Über den Priester von On, seinen Schwiegervater**
- **Stelle:** ~~1. Mose 50:4~~ → **1. Mose 50:4-5**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Why did Joseph speak unto the house of Pharaoh rather than to Pharaoh himself to ask leave?~~  
  → **Through whom did Joseph ask Pharaoh's leave to bury his father in Canaan?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Because Pharaoh was sick and unable to receive visitors~~  
  → **Through his brethren, who stood before Pharaoh**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Because he was in mourning apparel and unshaven for his father~~  
  → **Through the house of Pharaoh (his court)**
- **Option 2:** ~~Because Joseph had forgotten the court language of Egypt~~  
  → **Through Potiphar, the captain of the guard**
- **Option 3:** ~~Because he feared Pharaoh would arrest him for treason~~  
  → **Through the priest of On, his father-in-law**
- **Stelle:** ~~Genesis 50:4~~ → **Genesis 50:4-5**

---

#### `1mose_264`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Wo fand der schauerliche Bundesschluss zwischen den Stücken statt, bei dem Abram in einen tiefen Schlaf und dichte Finsternis fiel?~~  
  → **Bei welchem Ort wohnte Abram in der Zeit, als Gott mit ihm den Bund zwischen den Stücken schloss?**
- **Stelle:** ~~1. Mose 15:12~~ → **1. Mose 13:18; 14:13**
- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** Kapitel 15 nennt selbst keinen Ort. Nach 1. Mose 13:18 und 14:13 wohnte Abram in dieser Zeit bei den Äl-Bäumen Mamres zu Hebron.

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Where did the covenant between the pieces take place, when deep sleep and horror of darkness fell upon Abram?~~  
  → **Where did Abram dwell at the time when God made the covenant between the pieces with him?**
- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~At the plain of Mamre (in Hebron)~~  
  → **In the plain of Mamre (in Hebron)**
- **Option 1:** ~~At Haran in northern Mesopotamia~~  
  → **In Haran in Mesopotamia**
- **Option 2:** ~~At the city of Jerusalem (Salem)~~  
  → **In the city of Jerusalem (Salem)**
- **Option 3:** ~~At Gerar in land of the Philistines~~  
  → **In Gerar in the land of the Philistines**
- **Stelle:** ~~Genesis 15:12~~ → **Genesis 13:18; 14:13**
- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** Chapter 15 itself names no place. According to Genesis 13:18 and 14:13, Abram dwelt at that time in the plain of Mamre, which is in Hebron.

#### `1mose_276`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Nach christlicher (typologischer) Auslegung – Der Widder, den Abraham stellvertretend für Isaak opferte, war „mit seinen Hörnern im Gestrüpp verwickelt“. Welches neutestamentliche Schattenbild deutet sich hier an?~~  
  → **Nach christlicher (typologischer) Auslegung – Der Widder, den Abraham stellvertretend für Isaak opferte, war „mit den Hörnern in den Hecken verwickelt“. Welches neutestamentliche Schattenbild deutet sich hier an?**
- **Erklärung:** ~~Der anstelle Isaaks geopferte Widder ist ein alttestamentliches Vorbild auf das stellvertretende Sühnopfer Jesu Christi [ref:Johannes 1:29; Hebräer 10:10-14].~~  
  → **Der Widder hing mit den Hörnern im Dorngestrüpp fest; Dornen sind seit dem Sündenfall Zeichen des Fluches [ref:1. Mose 3:18], und Christus trug als Stellvertreter die Dornenkrone [ref:Johannes 19:2]. So weist der anstelle Isaaks geopferte Widder auf das stellvertretende Opfer Jesu Christi hin [ref:Hebräer 10:10-14].**

**EN**

- **Option 2:** ~~The golden crown worn by the kings of Judah when going to battle~~  
  → **The future trumpet (shofar) sounding at the judgment on Sinai**
- **Option 3:** ~~The brazen serpent lifted up upon a pole in the barren wilderness~~  
  → **The unbearable yoke of the Mosaic law**
- **Erklärung:** ~~The ram sacrificed in Isaac's stead is an Old Testament type of the substitutionary sacrifice of Jesus Christ [ref:John 1:29; Hebrews 10:10-14].~~  
  → **The ram was caught by its horns in a thicket; since the fall, thorns are a sign of the curse [ref:Genesis 3:18], and Christ as our substitute wore the crown of thorns [ref:John 19:2]. Thus the ram offered in Isaac's stead points to the substitutionary sacrifice of Jesus Christ [ref:Hebrews 10:10-14].**

- **Option 2:** ~~Moses and Aaron standing before Pharaoh demanding Israel's freedom~~  
  → **The two tables of the covenant from Sinai: the written law condemning the sinner, the blood of sacrifice granting grace**
- **Option 3:** ~~Peter and John healing the lame man at the beautiful temple gate~~  
  → **Israel and the heathen nations: the impenitent fall under judgment, while the elect find grace**

#### `1mose_282`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~According to Christian typological interpretation, why did God alone pass between the divided pieces in Genesis 15?~~  
  → **According to Christian typological interpretation, why did the sacrificial animals at Abram's covenant have to be divided exactly in two, and what did God's fiery passing between the pieces signify?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Abram had fallen asleep and forgotten the solemn covenant ritual~~  
  → **The dividing served only to burn the pieces faster upon the altar**
- **Option 1:** ~~Because human beings were forbidden to walk upon blood of sacrifice~~  
  → **Abram had to pass through to prove his complete sinlessness**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~To show that God alone bound Himself unilaterally to fulfill covenant~~  
  → **God alone passed through, taking the whole covenant obligation upon Himself as surety**
- **Option 3:** ~~To prove that Abram's seed would never suffer any earthly hardship~~  
  → **The dividing pictured the future division of the twelve tribes of Israel**

  → **Joseph married the heathen idol priestesses against God's will**
- **Option 2:** ~~He used his power to punish all pagan priests throughout the land~~  
  → **Joseph seized the throne with a mighty army of Hebrew mercenaries**

#### `1mose_284`

- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~The cross of Christ, through which blessing passeth by sovereign grace~~  
  → **That the highest blessing is bestowed not after the flesh, but by grace**
- **Option 1:** ~~The rejection of all Hebrew patriarchs in favour of Egyptian lords~~  
  → **Jacob was feeble with age and made a mistake which he later regretted**
- **Option 2:** ~~A royal priestly ritual that could only be performed by the aged~~  
  → **Crossing the arms was an ancient Egyptian rite to ward off evil spirits**
- **Option 3:** ~~A sign of physical weakness because Jacob could not lift his arms~~  
  → **It was to show that both brothers received exactly the same inheritance**

  → **Melchisedeks Brot und Wein werden typologisch als Vorbild auf das Mahl des Herrn gedeutet [ref:Lukas 22:19-20], sein Priestertum als Vorbild auf das ewige Priestertum Christi [ref:Hebräer 7:1-3].**

  → **Melchizedek's bread and wine are understood typologically as a figure of the Lord's supper [ref:Luke 22:19-20], and his priesthood as a figure of the eternal priesthood of Christ [ref:Hebrews 7:1-3].**

- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** Nach lutherischer und katholischer Zählung, die das Bilderverbot zum ersten Gebot rechnet, ist das Elterngebot das vierte und das Sabbatgebot das dritte Gebot.

  → **According to the Jewish and Reformed numbering, which of the Ten Commandments commands to honour father and mother with a promise?**
- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** In the Lutheran and Roman Catholic numbering, which counts the prohibition of images as part of the first commandment, honouring parents is the fourth commandment and the sabbath the third.

#### `hes_090`

**DE**

- **Stelle:** ~~Hesekiel 27:3~~ → **Hesekiel 27:3-5**
- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** Tyrus spricht in Vers 3: „Ich bin die Allerschönste“. In den Versen 4-5 wird die Stadt dann mit Bauleuten, Tafelwerk und Mastbäumen wie ein Schiff beschrieben.

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~How is Tyrus portrayed in the lamentation of Ezekiel 27:3?~~  
  → **How is Tyrus portrayed in Ezekiel 27:3-5?**
- **Option 0:** ~~As an invincible fortress set high upon a rock above the ocean waves~~  
  → **As a garden that beareth all the fruits of the earth**
- **Option 1:** ~~As a proud queen sitting upon an ivory throne adorned with pearls~~  
  → **As a fortress that no enemy can take**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~As a merchant ship situate at entry of sea, saying: I am perfect beauty~~  
  → **As a ship that saith: I am of perfect beauty**
- **Option 3:** ~~As a fruitful vineyard on the sea coast yielding choice purple grapes~~  
  → **As a temple wherein all nations pray**
- **Stelle:** ~~Ezekiel 27:3~~ → **Ezekiel 27:3-5**
- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** In verse 3 Tyrus says, "I am of perfect beauty." In verses 4-5 the city is then described as a ship, with builders, ship boards and masts.

- **Frage:** ~~What did the healed lame man do as he entered with Peter and John into the temple?~~  
  → **What bodily response did the lame man show immediately after his healing?**
- **Option 0:** ~~He fell down upon the ground and slept~~  
  → **He fell down and slept**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~He leaping up stood, and walked around~~  
  → **He leaped up, stood, and walked**
- **Option 2:** ~~He washed himself within the pool there~~  
  → **He washed himself in the pool**
- **Option 3:** ~~He made haste and returned unto his home~~  
  → **He hastened home at once**

---

#### `apg_014`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~An welchem Säulengang im Tempel lief das erstaunte Volk zu den Aposteln zusammen?~~  
  → **In welchem Säulengang im Tempel lief das erstaunte Volk zu den Aposteln zusammen?**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~In which porch of the temple did all the people run together greatly wondering?~~  
  → **In which porch of the temple did the people run together unto the apostles, greatly wondering?**
- **Option 0:** ~~In the court of Herod the king~~  
  → **In the porch of Herod**
- **Option 1:** ~~In the chamber of the priests~~  
  → **In the porch of the priests**
- **Option 3:** ~~In the hall of the chief ruler~~  
  → **In the porch of the king**

---

#### `apg_015`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Which religious party in Jerusalem was grieved that the apostles preached through Jesus the resurrection?~~  
  → **By what title did Peter call the Lord Jesus Christ before the people gathered in the temple?**
- **Option 0:** ~~The party of the Pharisees~~  
  → **The heir of the kingdoms**
- **Option 1:** ~~The Sadducees~~  
  → **The teacher of the law**
- **Option 2:** ~~The sect of the Essenes~~  
  → **The creator of the world**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~The party of the Herodians~~  
  → **The Prince of life**

---

#### `apg_016`

**DE**

- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~Die Verkündigung der Auferstehung der Toten in Jesus~~  
  → **Die Verkündigung der Auferstehung von den Toten in Jesus**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~How many in number were the men that believed the word preached by Peter?~~  
  → **Which part of the apostles' message caused the priests to arrest them?**
- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~About five thousand~~  
  → **The preaching through Jesus of the resurrection from the dead**
- **Option 1:** ~~About three thousand~~  
  → **The refusal of the tribute which Cæsar required of the Jews**
- **Option 2:** ~~About seven thousand~~  
  → **The destruction of the sanctuary, which they had announced in the name of Jesus**
- **Option 3:** ~~About twelve thousand~~  
  → **The sale of fields, whose price they had kept back from the temple**

---

#### `apg_017`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What did the rulers of the council perceive concerning Peter and John when they saw their boldness?~~  
  → **How great was the number of the men who believed after the speech in the temple?**
- **Option 0:** ~~That they were unlearned and ignorant men~~  
  → **About three thousand men**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~That they were rich scribes of the temple~~  
  → **About five thousand men**
- **Option 2:** ~~That they were soldiers of the king's guard~~  
  → **About ten thousand men**
- **Option 3:** ~~That they were foreign doctors from Babylon~~  
  → **About two thousand men**

---

#### `apg_018`

**DE**

- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~In keinem andern ist das Heil; kein anderer Name unter dem Himmel ist gegeben~~  
  → **In keinem anderen ist die Rettung; kein anderer Name unter dem Himmel ist gegeben**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What did Peter testify concerning salvation through the name of Jesus Christ?~~  
  → **What word did Peter declare before the council concerning the name of Jesus?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Salvation is attained by keeping the commandments of the law~~  
  → **All names are equally good, if one only believeth in God sincerely and from the heart**
- **Option 1:** ~~Salvation is promised through all the holy prophets of old~~  
  → **The law of Moses bringeth salvation, if one keepeth it exactly and with the whole heart**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Neither is there salvation in any other name under heaven~~  
  → **Neither is there salvation in any other: for there is none other name under heaven given**
- **Option 3:** ~~Salvation is given unto the seed of Abraham alone for ever~~  
  → **The temple secureth life, so long as one offereth there the prescribed sacrifices**

---

#### `apg_019`

**DE**

- **Option 0:** ~~Er ist zum Stolperstein geworden~~  
  → **Er ist zum Grenzstein geworden**
- **Option 2:** ~~Er ist zum Grundstein der Mauer geworden~~  
  → **Er ist zum Mühlstein geworden**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What did Peter and John answer when commanded not to speak at all in the name of Jesus?~~  
  → **According to Psalm 118, what is the stone become which was set at nought of the builders?**
- **Option 0:** ~~We will hold our peace until we depart unto the Gentiles~~  
  → **It is become a landmark stone**
- **Option 1:** ~~Whether it be right in the sight of God to hearken unto you, judge ye~~  
  → **It is become a stone of memorial**
- **Option 2:** ~~We will ask the high priest to examine our doctrine in full~~  
  → **It is become a millstone**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~We will appeal unto the Roman governor for our protection~~  
  → **It is become the head of the corner**

---

#### `apg_020`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Was bedeutet der Beiname Barnabas, den die Apostel dem Leviten Josef gaben?~~  
  → **Was bedeutet der Beiname Barnabas, den die Apostel dem Leviten Joses gaben?**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What happened to the place where the believers prayed after the apostles were let go?~~  
  → **What does the surname Barnabas mean, which the apostles gave unto the Levite Joses?**
- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~The place was shaken where they were assembled together~~  
  → **The son of consolation**
- **Option 1:** ~~A pillar of fire appeared above the roof of the chamber~~  
  → **The son of strength**
- **Option 2:** ~~A great company of angels descended upon the company~~  
  → **A man of peace**
- **Option 3:** ~~The doors of the chamber were opened by a mighty rushing wind~~  
  → **A man of God**

#### `apg_021`

**DE**

- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Hananias und Saphira~~  
  → **Ananias und Saphira**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What Levite of Cyprus sold land and laid the money at the apostles' feet?~~  
  → **Which married couple lied to the Holy Ghost at the sale of their land?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Stephen the deacon~~  
  → **Aquila and Priscilla**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Joses, surnamed Barnabas~~  
  → **Ananias and Sapphira**
- **Option 2:** ~~Philip the evangelist~~  
  → **Zacharias and Elisabeth**
- **Option 3:** ~~Mnason of the island~~  
  → **Felix and Drusilla**

#### `apg_022`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Welches Schicksal traf Hananias unmittelbar nach der Überführung durch Petrus?~~  
  → **Welches Schicksal traf Ananias unmittelbar nach der Überführung durch Petrus?**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What happened unto Ananias immediately after Peter convicted him of lying to the Holy Ghost?~~  
  → **What befell Ananias immediately after Peter had convicted him?**
- **Option 0:** ~~He was blinded before the whole church~~  
  → **He was struck blind before all the multitude**
- **Option 3:** ~~He was bound and put into the prison~~  
  → **He was forthwith put in prison**

---

#### `apg_023`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Welches Phänomen bewirkte Heilung, als Petrus durch die Straßen ging?~~  
  → **Was erhofften die Leute für die Kranken, die sie auf die Gassen trugen, wenn Petrus vorüberkam?**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~Dass wenigstens sein Schatten einen von ihnen überschatte~~  
  → **Dass auch nur sein Schatten einen von ihnen überschattete**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What miraculous benefit did the sick in Jerusalem hope for from Peter as he passed by?~~  
  → **What did the people hope for the sick whom they brought forth into the streets, when Peter passed by?**
- **Option 0:** ~~That they might touch the hem of his garment in faith~~  
  → **That his mantle, hanging over his shoulder, might touch them**
- **Option 1:** ~~At the least the shadow of Peter passing by might overshadow them~~  
  → **That his voice might call them as he passed by them**
- **Option 2:** ~~That he might anoint their foreheads with holy temple oil~~  
  → **That his look might fall on them as he beheld them kindly**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~That he might speak a word of blessing over their heads~~  
  → **That at the least his shadow might overshadow some of them**

---

#### `apg_024`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Who opened the prison doors by night and brought the apostles forth?~~  
  → **Who delivered the imprisoned apostles out of the common prison by night?**
- **Option 1:** ~~A faithful Roman guard~~  
  → **A Roman soldier**
- **Option 2:** ~~A great earthquake there~~  
  → **A great earthquake**
- **Option 3:** ~~A believer in the city~~  
  → **A believing servant**

---

#### `apg_025`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What famous principle did Peter and the other apostles proclaim before the council?~~  
  → **Which renowned doctor of the law counselled the council to be prudent?**
- **Option 0:** ~~We ought to obey God rather than men~~  
  → **Ezra, the scribe and priest**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~We must obey all the ordinances of the king~~  
  → **Gamaliel, a Pharisee and doctor of the law**
- **Option 2:** ~~The law of the fathers must be kept above all~~  
  → **Caiaphas, the high priest and son in law of Annas**
- **Option 3:** ~~Peace with all rulers is our holy calling~~  
  → **Nicodemus, the Pharisee and ruler of the Jews**

#### `apg_026`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Why did a murmuring of the Grecians against the Hebrews arise in the early church?~~  
  → **What dispute led to the appointment of the first seven deacons?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Because their widows were neglected in the daily ministration~~  
  → **A dispute over the circumcision of the Gentiles who had joined the church**
- **Option 1:** ~~Because their young men were not chosen to preach the word~~  
  → **A lack of money in the common treasury, which no one would fill any more**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Because they were forbidden to enter the inner courts of Zion~~  
  → **The widows of the Grecians were neglected in the daily ministration**
- **Option 3:** ~~Because they paid greater tribute money than the native Jews~~  
  → **A dispute over the sabbath, which some believers would keep more strictly**

#### `apg_027`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What qualifications did the apostles require for the seven men to be appointed over the business?~~  
  → **What was the decisive requirement in choosing the seven deacons?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Men of great earthly riches and having honorable kindred in Zion~~  
  → **They should be rich and noble and of honourable families**
- **Option 1:** ~~Men learned in the wisdom of Rome and speaking the Latin tongue~~  
  → **They should be learned at Rome and skilled in the Latin tongue**
- **Option 2:** ~~Men had in reputation with the priests and serving in the temple~~  
  → **They should be esteemed by the priests and serve in the temple**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~Men of honest report, full of the Holy Ghost and divine wisdom~~  
  → **They should be of honest report, full of the Holy Ghost and wisdom**

**DE**

- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~Stephanus, voll Gnade~~  
  → **Stephanus, voll Glauben und Kraft**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Which of the seven chosen men did great wonders and miracles among the people?~~  
  → **Which of the seven chosen deacons was distinguished by wonders?**
- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~Stephen, full of faith~~  
  → **Stephen, full of faith and power**

#### `apg_029`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What did the council behold concerning Stephen's face as they looked stedfastly on him?~~  
  → **What accusation did the witnesses who had been stirred up bring against Stephen?**
- **Option 0:** ~~His face was covered with a dark cloud of sorrow~~  
  → **He had stolen the temple treasure and kept back the offerings**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~They saw his face as it had been the face of an angel~~  
  → **He spake blasphemous words against this holy place, and the law**
- **Option 2:** ~~His countenance was pale as ashes through great fear~~  
  → **He had joined himself to the Romans and betrayed the people**
- **Option 3:** ~~His face shone with the brightness of the noon day sun~~  
  → **He had withheld the tithe and broken the feasts of the law**

#### `apg_030`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~In Stephen's defense, unto whom did the God of glory appear in Mesopotamia?~~  
  → **How did Stephen's face appear before the assembled council?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Unto our father Abraham~~  
  → **As a sunlit flame**
- **Option 1:** ~~Unto Noah the righteous~~  
  → **As a dark cloud**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Unto Isaac on the mount~~  
  → **As it had been the face of an angel**
- **Option 3:** ~~Unto Jacob at Bethel~~  
  → **As a terrible countenance**

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~An welchem Ort erschien Gott dem Erzvater Abraham vor der Siedlung in Haran?~~

- **Option 1:** ~~In Kanaan, ehe er nach Ägypten zog~~  
  → **In Damaskus, ehe er nach Ägypten zog**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What did God promise to give unto Abraham for a possession, when as yet he had no child?~~  
  → **Where, according to Stephen's speech, did the God of glory appear unto the patriarch Abraham?**
- **Option 0:** ~~He promised that he would give it to him for a possession~~  
  → **In Egypt, before he dwelt in Canaan**
- **Option 1:** ~~All the silver and gold of the kings of Mesopotamia~~  
  → **In Damascus, before he went down into Egypt**
- **Option 2:** ~~A kingdom in the wilderness of Sinai for all his days~~  
  → **In Beer-sheba, before he dwelt in Gerar**

  → **In Mesopotamia, before he dwelt in Charran**

  → **Seven hundred years**
- **Option 2:** ~~Two hundred years only~~

  → **Three hundred years**

  → **Which son of Jacob was sold into Egypt by his brethren out of envy?**

  → **Reuben, the firstborn son**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Joseph, envied of all~~

  → **Judah, the fourth son of Jacob**

**EN**

  → **Nicanor**

  → **Elymas the sorcerer**

- **Option 0:** ~~The way from Jerusalem unto Gaza, which is desert~~  
  → **He was the queen's captain and commanded her troops**

  → **The book of the Psalms of David**

  → **The book of the prophet Esaias**

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Was veranlasste den Kämmerer aus Äthiopien zur Bitte um die Taufe am Wege?~~

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What confession of faith did the Ethiopian eunuch make before his baptism?~~  
  → **What did the eunuch of Ethiopia see on the way, so that he asked to be baptized?**

**EN**

  → **Near Jerusalem**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~At Caesarea~~  
  → **Near Damascus**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Unto what city was Saul journeying when a light from heaven shined round about him?~~

  → **For seven days**

  → **For forty days**

  → **Ananias, ein Jünger in Damaskus**

**EN**

- **Option 1:** ~~Ananias~~  
  → **Disguised as a Roman soldier through the main gate**

- **Option 3:** ~~Silas~~  
  → **Under the protection of the Roman guards**

  → **Tabitha, was übersetzt «Gazelle» heißt**

  → **Which believing woman of Joppa, full of good works, did Peter raise from the dead through prayer?**

  → **Lydia, the seller of purple**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Tabitha, called Dorcas~~

  → **Rhoda, the young damsel of the house**

  → **Governor of the province of Judæa**
- **Option 1:** ~~Priest in an idol temple of Rome~~

  → **A garment of fine linen from heaven**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~A ladder set upon earth, reaching unto the gate of heaven~~

**EN**

  → **Three men**
- **Option 1:** ~~Behold, five men seek thee~~  
  → **Five men**
- **Option 2:** ~~Behold, two men seek thee~~

- **Option 3:** ~~Behold, seven men seek thee~~

#### `apg_049`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What happened while Peter yet spake unto Cornelius and his household in Caesarea?~~  
  → **What happened to the Gentile hearers during Peter's sermon at Cæsarea?**

  → **That he had taken money from the church's treasury for himself**

  → **That he had broken the sabbath and worked on the feast day**

  → **That he had left Jerusalem and forsaken the church**

- **Option 3:** ~~Matthew, who was a publican~~  
  → **Matthew, the former publican**
- 💬 **Erklärung neu:** James the brother of John had already been killed (Acts 12:2). The James who speaks here is the Lord's brother, whom Paul counts among the apostles (Galatians 1:19; 2:9).

  → **Auf das Zeugnis Gottes, das er ihnen verkündigte**

- **Option 1:** ~~Upon the mighty signs of the Holy Ghost sent down~~

- **Option 2:** ~~Upon the revelation of Jesus Christ from heaven~~  
  → **Upon Jesus Christ, and him crucified**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~With enticing words of man's wisdom and eloquence~~

- **Stelle:** ~~1 Corinthians 2:4-5~~ → **1 Corinthians 2:1-5**

**DE**

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Because he hath not diligently searched the statutes of Moses~~  
  → **Because the law forbiddeth him, so that he may not receive nor understand them**
- **Option 2:** ~~Because the almighty Lord hath purposely blinded his understanding~~  
  → **Because he hath no scriptures nor teachers to learn the truth**

  → **Because evil powers hinder him, so that spiritual things remain hid from him**

  → **What mind have the spiritual believers according to the words of the second chapter?**
- **Option 0:** ~~The secret traditions of the ancient elders~~  
  → **The mind of the ancients**
- **Option 1:** ~~The civil authority of Roman jurisdiction~~  
  → **The mind of the philosophers**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Because we have received the mind of Christ~~  
  → **They have the mind of Christ**
- **Option 3:** ~~A great measure of worldly disputation~~  
  → **The mind of the doctors of the law**

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~As unto perfect elders established in the faith~~  
  → **As unto the righteous and perfect, already grown up in the faith**

  → **As unto the wise and learned, possessing the wisdom of the world**
- **Option 2:** ~~As unto holy prophets possessing deep understanding~~  
  → **As unto enemies of the gospel, that had wholly cast away the faith**

#### `1kor_012`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Welches Bild aus der Landwirtschaft gebraucht Paulus für Seinen Dienst und den des Apollos?~~  
  → **Welches Bild aus der Landwirtschaft gebraucht Paulus für seinen Dienst und den des Apollos?**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What division of labour between ministers is declared in 1 Corinthians 3:6-7?~~  
  → **What figure from husbandry doth Paul use for his own ministry and that of Apollos?**
- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~I have planted, Apollos watered; but God gave the increase~~  
  → **Paul planted, Apollos watered; but God gave the increase**
- **Option 1:** ~~Paul gathered tithes, Apollos built sanctuaries and altars~~  
  → **Paul reaped, Apollos sowed; but God gave the fruit**
- **Option 2:** ~~Apollos governed the church, while Paul served as deacon~~  
  → **Paul ploughed, Apollos mowed; but God gave the weather**
- **Option 3:** ~~Ministers accomplish all things by their own strength~~  
  → **Paul gathered, Apollos sold; but God gave the gain**

  → **Durch das Urteil eines römischen Richters**
- **Option 1:** ~~Marmor, Granit und gebrannte Ziegel~~  
  → **Durch die Abstimmung der versammelten Brüder**

  → **By the judgment of a Roman magistrate**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Because it shall be revealed by fire~~  
  → **By the fire, which shall try every man's work**
- **Option 3:** ~~By the weighing of worldly riches~~  
  → **By the weighing of earthly riches**

#### `1kor_015`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What solemn truth concerning the church is declared in 1 Corinthians 3:16?~~  
  → **What dignity doth the apostle Paul ascribe unto the fellowship of the believers?**
- **Option 0:** ~~They are an earthly province under the Roman government~~  
  → **They are a worldly council that decideth the matters of the city**
- **Option 1:** ~~They are a philosophical school of disputing scholars~~  
  → **They are a school of Rome wherein the citizens learn wisdom**
- **Option 2:** ~~They are a secret league practicing mystical ceremonies~~  
  → **They are servants of kings, owing them obedience and tribute**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~Ye are the temple of God, and the Spirit of God dwelleth in you~~  
  → **They are the temple of God, and the Spirit of God dwelleth in them**

#### `1kor_016`

**DE**

- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~Als Diener Christi und Haushalter über Gottes Geheimnisse~~  
  → **Als Untergebene Christi und Verwalter der Geheimnisse Gottes**

**EN**

- **Option 1:** ~~As lords over the faith and temporal rulers of the assemblies~~  
  → **As lords over the faith, whom the church must obey**
- **Option 2:** ~~As common philosophers disputing about the origins of creation~~  
  → **As judges of the church, that decide guilt and innocence**
- **Option 3:** ~~As priests offering animal sacrifices upon the holy temple altar~~  
  → **As kings of this world, that reign in the name of Christ**

#### `1kor_018`

**EN**

  → **What judging should believers avoid before the coming of the Lord?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Condemn every errant brother immediately without mercy~~  
  → **To examine their own works before the Lord come to judgment**
- **Option 1:** ~~Deliver all accused brethren unto the Roman magistrates~~  
  → **To judge the doctrines that are spread abroad in the church**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Therefore judge nothing before the time, until the Lord come~~  
  → **To judge before the time, until the Lord come**
- **Option 3:** ~~Establish human courts within the assembly to pass sentence~~  
  → **To chasten the sinners that are in the church**

#### `1kor_019`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Unto what are the apostles made according to the description in 1 Corinthians 4:9?~~  
  → **What figure describeth the hard and despised estate of the apostles in the world?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Unto kings reigning in glory over the provinces of the earth~~  
  → **A glorious triumph, wherein the multitude shouteth unto them**
- **Option 1:** ~~Unto wealthy masters receiving honour from all the nations~~  
  → **A mighty host in the field, that putteth all enemies to flight**
- **Option 2:** ~~Unto doctors of wisdom revered by the philosophers of Greece~~  
  → **A royal court, admired of all the nations**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~For we are made a spectacle unto the world, and to angels, and to men~~  
  → **A spectacle unto the world, and to angels, and to men**

#### `1kor_021`

**EN**

  → **That one stole the goods of the church**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~That one should have his father's wife, not so much as named among the Gentiles~~  
  → **That one should have his father's wife**
- **Option 2:** ~~That members worshipped idols openly within the heathen temples~~  
  → **That one refused the oath of allegiance**
- **Option 3:** ~~That deacons defrauded the poor of the alms collected for them~~  
  → **That one served idols**

#### `1kor_022`

**EN**

  → **To lay a fine upon him, that it might profit and help the church**

  → **To lay upon him a fast of three years, until he repented before the church**

  → **To send him unto another city, that he might begin a new life there**

**EN**

  → **A muddy spring fouleth the whole brook**

**EN**

  → **With such an one no not to eat**
- **Option 2:** ~~Deliver him unto the civil courts to be punished by the law~~  
  → **To give him a gift unto repentance**

**EN**

  → **They demanded too much money and went to law out of covetousness**

  → **They went to law before the unbelievers, and not before the saints**

- **Option 2:** ~~The saints shall minister as consecrated priests in Jerusalem~~  
  → **They shall keep and govern the sanctuary**

- **Option 0:** ~~Because civil imperial statutes forbid all unchaste deeds within the city~~  
  → **They belong unto the state**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~Your bodies are members of Christ, and must not be made members of an harlot~~  
  → **They are the members of Christ**
- **Option 2:** ~~Because it consumeth bodily strength and shorteneth the days of a man's youth~~  
  → **They are instruments of the world**
- **Option 3:** ~~Because heathen neighbours will mock the holy assemblies of the congregation~~

#### `1kor_030`

**DE**

- **Frage:** ~~Wozu ist der leibliche Körper des Glaubenden nach den Schlussversen von Kapitel 6 geweiht?~~  
  → **Was ist der Leib des Glaubenden nach den Schlussversen von Kapitel 6?**

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~Why are believers commanded to glorify God in their body and in their spirit?~~  
  → **What is the body of the believer according to the closing verses of chapter 6?**
- **Option 0:** ~~Because the physical frame is formed of dust and perisheth forevermore~~  
  → **The body is the dwelling of the soul, which returneth unto God after death**
- **Option 1:** ~~Because human discipline meriteth justification before the righteous God~~  
  → **The body is a house of earthly treasures, which are gotten here**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~The body is the temple of the Holy Ghost, and ye are bought with a price~~  
  → **The body is the temple of the Holy Ghost which is in you**
- **Option 3:** ~~Because bodily glory gaineth the favour and praise of the Roman senate~~  
  → **The body is a place of worldly feasts, wherein men rejoice**

#### `1kor_031`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What duty of mutual benevolence is enjoined upon husband and wife in marriage?~~  
  → **What principle doth Paul lay down concerning the duties of marriage?**
- **Option 0:** ~~The wife must abide in perpetual silence and never speak to her husband~~  
  → **The husband ruleth without regard, and the wife obeyeth him in all things**
- **Option 1:** ~~The husband may put away his wife at his pleasure for any displeasure~~  
  → **The wife hath no rights in the house, but serveth the husband only**
- **Option 2:** ~~Spouses should separate perpetually to pursue ascetic contemplation~~  
  → **Abstinence is always required, even against the will of the spouse**

---

#### `1kor_032`

**EN**

- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~Let not the wife depart from her husband; or else remain unmarried or reconciled~~  
  → **Let not the wife depart from her husband, and let not the husband put away his wife**
- **Option 1:** ~~Husbands and wives may divorce at their own pleasure and remarry whom they will~~  
  → **Divorce is free at any time, if both husband and wife agree thereto and will it**
- **Option 2:** ~~The wife must be put away immediately if she bringeth forth no male children~~  
  → **Marriage holdeth but seven years, and afterward it may be loosed at will**
- **Option 3:** ~~Marriage is made void on the day of baptism and hath no more binding authority~~  
  → **To marry again is always easily allowed, if the first spouse hath gone away**

**EN**

  → **He is condemned forthwith, because he refuseth the faith of his spouse**
- **Option 2:** ~~The believer must depart from the household and live alone~~  
  → **He must leave the house, because he troubleth the faith of the family**
- **Option 3:** ~~The unbelieving partner must be delivered unto church discipline~~  
  → **He loseth all rights to the children, because he is not sanctified**

---

#### `1kor_034`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Every believer must forsake his earthly occupation and become a preacher~~  
  → **Let every man change his trade forthwith, that he may serve God better**
- **Option 1:** ~~Every bondsman must purchase his freedom immediately with borrowed gold~~  
  → **Let every bondservant flee, that he may live free in the Lord**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Let every man abide in the same calling wherein he was called with God~~  
  → **Let every man abide in the same calling wherein he was called**
- **Option 3:** ~~Every citizen must relocate his dwelling unto the city of Jerusalem~~  
  → **Let every man go into the wilderness, that he may serve God alone**

#### `1kor_037`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Heathen idols possess real divine authority over their appointed provinces~~  
  → **There are a thousand real gods, which rule over every city and every house**

  → **Idols direct the lot of the men that call upon them and worship them**
- **Option 3:** ~~Every graven image contains a living soul created by the almighty Lord~~  
  → **Idols are mightier than the angels, if men but worship them diligently**

#### `1kor_039`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~When does the liberty of a mature believer become an occasion of sin?~~  
  → **When doth the use of a liberty lawful in itself become sin against Christ?**
- **Option 0:** ~~When he studyeth the holy scriptures in his own private chamber~~  
  → **When one bringeth no sacrifice for it**
- **Option 1:** ~~When he bestoweth alms upon the poor without consulting the elders~~  
  → **When one will not fast and pray thereby**
- **Option 2:** ~~When he preacheth the gospel unto the uncircumcised Gentiles~~  
  → **When the elders expressly forbid it**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~Lest by any means this liberty of yours become a stumblingblock to them that are weak~~  
  → **When it becometh a stumblingblock to them that are weak**

#### `1kor_040`

**EN**

- **Option 0 ✅:** ~~Wherefore, if meat make my brother to offend, I will eat no flesh while the world standeth~~  
  → **To eat no flesh while the world standeth, lest he make his brother to offend**
- **Option 1:** ~~He will refrain from preaching the gospel in the presence of the weak~~  
  → **Never again to journey unto Corinth, that he might avoid strife**
- **Option 2:** ~~He will compel the weak brother to conform unto his superior knowledge~~  
  → **Never again to be circumcised, that he might offend no man**
- **Option 3:** ~~He will withdraw from the fellowship of the church and dwell alone~~  
  → **To minister only in the temple, that he might give no offence there**

**DE**

  → **Auf das Recht, Älteste in den Gemeinden einzusetzen**

- **Option 0:** ~~The authority to baptize converts in the name of the Lord Jesus~~  
  → **The right to preach in the church**
- **Option 1 ✅:** ~~The right to eat and to drink, and to forbear working for his maintenance~~  
  → **The right to receive his living from the church**
- **Option 2:** ~~The privilege of carrying scrolls of the holy law into the assembly~~  
  → **The right to write epistles unto the churches**

  → **The right to ordain elders in the churches**

#### `1kor_042`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Thou shalt build a fence around the roof of thy house for safety~~  
  → **Thou shalt not steal, neither rob thy neighbour**
- **Option 3:** ~~Thou shalt release thy debtor after the passing of seven counted years~~  
  → **Thou shalt keep the sabbath holy, and do no work therein**

#### `1kor_043`

**EN**

- **Frage:** ~~What was Paul's reward and boasting in preaching the gospel of Christ?~~  
  → **What doth Paul name as his particular glorying in the preaching of the gospel?**
- **Option 0:** ~~That he received great wealth and possession from the assemblies~~  
  → **That he wrought many miracles and healed the sick**
- **Option 1:** ~~That he attained unto high honour and title among all the churches~~  
  → **That he founded many cities and gathered great churches**
- **Option 2:** ~~That he was exempted from all bodily persecution and suffering~~  
  → **That all men praised him and the church held him in high honour**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~That, when I preach the gospel, I may make the gospel of Christ without charge~~  
  → **That he preached the gospel without charge**

#### `1kor_045`

**EN**

  → **The race in the stadium for the victor's crown**

  → **Throwing contests upon the field for the city's wreath**

#### `1kor_050`

**DE**

- **Option 1:** ~~Sich allen Bräuchen anpassen~~  
  → **Sich allein nach den Bräuchen der Väter richten**

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Seek first one's own pleasure and comfort in all circumstances~~  
  → **To seek always one's own profit**
- **Option 1:** ~~Conform strictly unto the social customs of the Roman capital~~  
  → **To walk only after the customs of the fathers**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Whether therefore ye eat, or drink, or whatsoever ye do, do all to the glory of God~~  
  → **To do all to the glory of God**
- **Option 3:** ~~Follow the dietary traditions established by the elders of Israel~~

#### `1kor_051`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~The head of the church is the Roman Caesar, and the head of man is the magistrate~~  
  → **The man is the head of God, and the woman the head of the man**
- **Option 1:** ~~The head of every family is the high priest, and the head of woman is the deacon~~  
  → **The woman is the head of the man, and the man the head of Christ**
- **Option 2:** ~~There is no order of headship in the kingdom of God, but absolute equality of office~~  
  → **No man hath a head, but all are equal before God**

**DE**

**EN**

- **Option 1:** ~~Because civil laws of Corinth compelled women to wear veils in public assemblies~~

- **Option 2:** ~~Because only veiled persons were permitted to enter into the holy sanctuary~~  
  → **Because the law of Moses commandeth every woman to be veiled**
- **Option 3:** ~~To conceal her face from the gaze of the ruling elders and foreign visitors~~  
  → **Because it was a Roman custom, which every honest woman observed**

#### `1kor_053`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Offering animal sacrifices upon the holy communion table before the brethren~~  
  → **They sold the bread unto strangers and kept the price for themselves**

  → **They withheld the wine which the church needed for the supper**
- **Option 3:** ~~Demanding payment of admission from the poor before breaking the bread~~  
  → **They bade no strangers and shut them out from the supper**

#### `1kor_054`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~Take, eat; this is an emblem of the deliverance out of the land of Egypt~~  
  → **This is a token of the love which I shew unto you**
- **Option 1:** ~~Take, eat; this is the reward of your faithful attendance upon the sanctuary~~  
  → **Eat this bread in fasting, until I come again**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Take, eat: this is my body, which is broken for you: this do in remembrance of me~~  
  → **This is my body, which is broken for you**
- **Option 3:** ~~Take, eat; this is the bread of sorrow which our ancient fathers did eat~~  
  → **This bread bringeth a good harvest, which I promise you**

#### `1kor_055`

**DE**

- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~Er isst und trinkt sich selbst zum Gericht~~  
  → **Er isst und trinkt sich selbst ein Gericht**

  → **He loseth his goods and is cast out of the church**
- **Option 1:** ~~He must pay a heavy monetary penalty unto the ruling elders of the church~~  
  → **He is banished from the city and may not return**
- **Option 2:** ~~He is arrested by the civil magistrates and cast into the public prison~~  
  → **He may never again pray in the temple nor enter the sanctuary**
- **Option 3 ✅:** ~~He eateth and drinketh damnation to himself, not discerning the Lord's body~~  
  → **He eateth and drinketh damnation to himself**

#### `1kor_056`

**EN**

- **Option 1:** ~~That Moses is the prophet~~  
  → **That Jesus is a great prophet**
- **Option 2:** ~~That the law is perpetual~~  
  → **That God is the Creator of the world**
- **Option 3:** ~~That the temple is holy~~  
  → **That the law is holy**

---

#### `1kor_058`

**DE**

- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~Wir sind durch einen Geist alle zu einem Leib getauft~~  
  → **Wir wurden alle in einem Geist zu einem Leib getauft**

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~By signing an imperial covenant under the authority of the magistrate~~  
  → **By fulfilling the law, which is laid upon all believers**
- **Option 1:** ~~By taking an oath of allegiance unto the ruling elders of Jerusalem~~  
  → **By dwelling in one city and having all things common**
- **Option 2 ✅:** ~~For by one Spirit are we all baptized into one body, whether Jews or Gentiles~~  
  → **For by one Spirit are we all baptized into one body**
- **Option 3:** ~~By contributing equal portions of money unto the treasury of the saints~~  
  → **By the decree of the apostles, which ordered all into one church**

#### `1kor_059`

**EN**

- **Option 0:** ~~The body would attain unto absolute perfection and divine majesty~~  
  → **The body would be exceeding strong**
- **Option 1:** ~~The body would be delivered from all disease and mortal infirmity~~  
  → **The body would need no meat**

  → **A green horse; vesture of glistening silver cloth**
